# RobotDSL component library assets

The meshes, pictures and walking controllers of the parts of the RobotDSL component library, which the RobotDSL
Studio draws and drives. Each part's files are in `parts/<id>/`, and each walker's, the walking controller of a robot
put together from the library's parts, in `walkers/<id>/`; `examples/` holds pictures of the library's example robots.

## What is here

- `parts/<id>/assets/`: the part's mesh, and a legged robot's walking controller. The mesh is made from a model of
  the part: its maker's, another's (LDraw's for LEGO parts, MuJoCo Menagerie's for its robots), or one the library
  draws from the maker's pictures and dimensions, where the maker publishes none or one the library cannot use.
- `parts/<id>/images/`: pictures of the part, drawn from its mesh.
- `parts/<id>/source/`: what the part was made from, as it was published: models, pictures and data sheets, and the
  licence and notice texts that came with them, kept for research purposes (below).
- `walkers/<id>/assets/`: a walker's policy, trained for a robot of a body and legs that are parts of the library,
  and `walkers/<id>/source/`, the licence and notice texts that came with it and with the robot's model.

## For research

The files under `parts/<id>/source/` are kept for research purposes: to show, and to check, what each part was made
from. Most are as their makers or authors published them; some were prepared here from what they published, a model
composed from a maker's files for instance, as the part's README says. What a maker or author published remains theirs
and is under the terms it was published with, and this dataset grants no further rights in it. The source files of the
parts of the library's own design, whose maker is the RobotDSL Team, are the team's and are under the licence the table
gives them.

## Licences

The table gives, for each part, its maker, its product page, where its model comes from and the licence the library
records for that model, "Not stated" where it records none, with a link to the licence's text. Where a licence or
notice text came with the model, it is beside the part's files, under `parts/<id>/source/`. The notes after the
table say, for each part whose model is licensed, how its mesh was made from the model, and give the credits a
licence asks for. Product names and trademarks are their owners'.

## Walkers

The table gives, for each walker, the robot it walks, that robot's maker and product page, where its policy comes
from and the licence the library records for the policy, with a link to its text. The robot is put together from the
library's parts, whose rows are above.

| Walker | Name | Maker | Product page | Policy | Licence | Licence texts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `robotdsl_biped_walker` | RobotDSL Biped's walking policy | RobotDSL Team | Not stated | [creativecommons.org](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/) | [CC-BY-4.0](https://spdx.org/licenses/CC-BY-4.0.html) |  |
| `unitree_g1_walker` | Unitree G1's walking policy | Unitree Robotics | [www.unitree.com](https://www.unitree.com/g1) | [github.com](https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground) | [CC-BY-4.0](https://spdx.org/licenses/CC-BY-4.0.html) | [LICENSE.menagerie](walkers/unitree_g1_walker/source/LICENSE.menagerie), [LICENSE.playground](walkers/unitree_g1_walker/source/LICENSE.playground) |

## Notes on the licensed models

### `booster_t1`

- Model: MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/t1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `booster_t1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Booster Robotics' own description. Every number of the part is read from that model as MuJoCo compiles it: each body below the root `Trunk` is a link on the hinge its servo turns, with its mass, centre of mass and inertia, its hinge's axis, range, damping, armature and friction, and its servo's stiffness and torque limit; the servos hold the angles of its keyframe `home`, which the policy starts from.

### `hello_robot_pg4_parallel_gripper`

- Model: https://github.com/hello-robot/stretch4_urdf/tree/6aa4658402e694be0cf9caa51d5e86ea7650f340, `stretch4_urdf/SE4_tools/eoa_wrist_dw4_tool_pg4/meshes/pjg_body_link.STL`, `finger_right_link.STL`, `aruco_fingertip_right_link.STL`, `finger_left_link.STL`, `aruco_fingertip_left_link.STL`, meshes of the maker's robot description, joined as its joints place them; reduced at 20126 triangles, from [-0.0, -71.4, -31.2] to [166.9, 71.4, 31.2] mm in the part frame.

### `unitree_g1`

- Model: MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_g1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description. Every number of the part is read from that model as MuJoCo compiles it: each body below the root `pelvis` is a link on the hinge its servo turns, with its mass, centre of mass and inertia, its hinge's axis, range, damping, armature and friction, and its servo's stiffness and torque limit; the servos hold the angles of its keyframe `knees_bent`, which the policy starts from.

### `unitree_g1_body`

- Model: the component library's `unitree_g1` without its legs, which are parts of their own (`unitree_g1_leg_left`, `unitree_g1_leg_right`). Its mesh, its hull, its connector `back` and the links of its waist, torso and arms are `unitree_g1`'s, whose model is MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_g1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description.

### `unitree_g1_leg_left`

- Model: the component library's `unitree_g1`, its links `left_hip_pitch_link`, `left_hip_roll_link`, `left_hip_yaw_link`, `left_knee_link`, `left_ankle_pitch_link`, `left_ankle_roll_link` as `unitree_g1` states them, each without its prefix here, the first one's hinge at the leg's frame. Its foot is `unitree_g1`'s `left_foot`. The model of `unitree_g1` is MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_g1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description.

### `unitree_g1_leg_right`

- Model: the component library's `unitree_g1`, its links `right_hip_pitch_link`, `right_hip_roll_link`, `right_hip_yaw_link`, `right_knee_link`, `right_ankle_pitch_link`, `right_ankle_roll_link` as `unitree_g1` states them, each without its prefix here, the first one's hinge at the leg's frame. Its foot is `unitree_g1`'s `right_foot`. The model of `unitree_g1` is MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_g1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description.

### `unitree_go1`

- Model: MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/go1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_go1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description. ### `robotdsl_biped_walker`

- Model: the RobotDSL Team's policy for the RobotDSL Biped, trained (301,957,120 steps, seed 1) by tools/train_walker.py at 549242fb654e on RobotDSL-Core's model of the robot, standing, by MuJoCo Playground's G1 joystick task at d59156e099a8, changed to stand still when told nothing, reward_config.scales.joint_deviation_hip -0.05, reward_config.scales.tracking_ang_vel 2.0, the policy its own mirror image, as the robot is; CC-BY-4.0. Its files are published under CC-BY-4.0 (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/), crediting the RobotDSL Team.

### `unitree_g1_walker`

- Model: the policy of the component library's `unitree_g1`'s walking controller, of which `unitree_g1` says: The RobotDSL Team's policy for the Unitree G1, trained (401,571,840 steps, seed 1) by MuJoCo Playground's joystick task at d59156e099a8, changed as its T1 task is to stand still when told nothing, reward_config.tracking_sigma 0.2, reward_config.scales.tracking_lin_vel 1.5, reward_config.scales.tracking_ang_vel 1.5, on its feet-only model, which this part states; CC-BY-4.0. The model of `unitree_g1` is MuJoCo Playground's feet-only model of the robot, `mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1/xmls/` in https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground at d59156e099a8785dd58fbce2e25a6ee1f7b5800c, the model its joystick policy was trained on, made from MuJoCo Menagerie's `unitree_g1` (https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_menagerie at 0059d4335f8156206f63a35662313385f7ad6d74), which Menagerie derives from Unitree Robotics' own description.
